    });
    addEventListener('blur', () => {
      firing = false;
      dragLooking = false;
      clearInputKeys();
    });
    addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
      if (!pointerLockedToGame && !(dragLookMode && dragLooking)) return;
      if (paused) return;
      player.yaw -= e.movementX*activeMouseSensitivity;
      player.pitch += e.movementY*activeMousePitchScale;
      player.pitch = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(player.pitch, -1.22, 1.22);
    });
    addEventListener('contextmenu', e => e.preventDefault());

    function handlePointerLockChange() {
      if (usingMobileControls() || !started) return;
      const lockElement = activePointerLockElement(), locked = !!lockElement;
      pointerLockedToGame = lockElement===renderer.domElement;
      document.body.classList.toggle('mouse-locked', locked);
      lockRequestPending = false;
      lockRequestToken++;
      firing = false;
      setAiming(false);
      clearInputKeys();

      if (locked) {
        pointerLockWasActive = true;
        explicitPointerUnlock = false;
        dragLookMode = false;
        renderer.domElement.style.cursor = '';
        enterGameplay();
        return;
      }

      const lostLiveLock = pointerLockWasActive;
      pointerLockWasActive = false;
      if (gameOver) return;

      if (explicitPointerUnlock) {
        explicitPointerUnlock = false;
        return; // setPause/endGame already selected the correct screen state.
      }

      // Only Escape/loss of a lock that was actually active pauses the match.
      // Initial request failures must not leave the simulation frozen.
      if (lostLiveLock && !dragLookMode) {
        paused = true;playActionSfx('pauseOpen');
        pauseScreen.style.display = 'grid';
      }
    }

    function handlePointerLockError() {
      const token = lockRequestToken;
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (token !== lockRequestToken || activePointerLockElement()) return;
        lockRequestPending = false;
        pointerLockedToGame=false;document.body.classList.remove('mouse-locked');
        if (!usingMobileControls() && started && !gameOver && !dragLookMode) {
          safeConsoleWarn('CatStrike:', lockDiagnostics(null));
          enableDragLookFallback();
        }
      }, 100);
    }

    document.addEventListener('pointerlockchange', handlePointerLockChange);
    document.addEventListener('mozpointerlockchange', handlePointerLockChange);
    document.addEventListener('webkitpointerlockchange', handlePointerLockChange);
    document.addEventListener('pointerlockerror', handlePointerLockError);
    document.addEventListener('mozpointerlockerror', handlePointerLockError);
    document.addEventListener('webkitpointerlockerror', handlePointerLockError);

    // Mobile movement joystick — independent pointer capture allows moving,
    // aiming, and firing simultaneously with three separate fingers.
    const stickBase = $('stickBase'), stickKnob = $('stickKnob');
    const moveStick = new THREE.Vector2();
    let stickPointer = null,stickCenterX=0,stickCenterY=0,stickMax=1;
    function refreshStickGeometry(){const r=stickBase.getBoundingClientRect();stickCenterX=r.left+r.width*.5;stickCenterY=r.top+r.height*.5;stickMax=Math.max(1e-6,r.width*.34);}
    function updateStick(e) {
      let dx = e.clientX-stickCenterX, dy = e.clientY-stickCenterY;
      const max = stickMax;
      if(Number.isFinite(dx)&&Number.isFinite(dy)&&Number.isFinite(max)&&max>1e-150&&max<1e150&&Math.abs(dx)<1e150&&Math.abs(dy)<1e150){
        if(dx*dx+dy*dy>max*max){const len=Math.hypot(dx,dy);dx*=max/len;dy*=max/len;}
      }else{const len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;if(len>max){dx*=max/len;dy*=max/len;}}
      moveStick.set(dx/max, dy/max);
      stickKnob.style.transform = `translate(${dx}px,${dy}px)`;
    }
    stickBase.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); stickPointer=e.pointerId; refreshStickGeometry(); try{stickBase.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_){} updateStick(e); });
    stickBase.addEventListener('pointermove', e => { if(e.pointerId===stickPointer){e.preventDefault();updateStick(e);} });
    const releaseStick = e => { if(e.pointerId===stickPointer){ stickPointer=null; moveStick.set(0,0); stickKnob.style.transform=''; } };
    stickBase.addEventListener('pointerup', releaseStick);
    stickBase.addEventListener('pointercancel', releaseStick);
    stickBase.addEventListener('lostpointercapture', releaseStick);

    // Mobile look: drag the world with a separate pointer. ADS automatically
    // lowers sensitivity for precise sniper aiming.
    let lookPointer = null, lookLastX=0, lookLastY=0;
    renderer.domElement.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {
      if (!usingMobileControls() || !started || paused || buyMenuOpen) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      if (lookPointer !== null) return;
      lookPointer = e.pointerId; lookLastX=e.clientX; lookLastY=e.clientY;
      try { renderer.domElement.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (_) {}
    });
    renderer.domElement.addEventListener('pointermove', e => {
      if (e.pointerId !== lookPointer || paused || buyMenuOpen) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      let dx=e.clientX-lookLastX,dy=e.clientY-lookLastY;if(dx>70)dx=70;else if(dx<-70)dx=-70;if(dy>70)dy=70;else if(dy<-70)dy=-70;
      lookLastX=e.clientX; lookLastY=e.clientY;
      player.yaw -= dx*activeTouchSensitivity;
      player.pitch += dy*activeTouchPitchScale;
      player.pitch=THREE.MathUtils.clamp(player.pitch,-1.22,1.22);
    });
    const endLook=e=>{if(e.pointerId===lookPointer)lookPointer=null;};
    renderer.domElement.addEventListener('pointerup',endLook);
    renderer.domElement.addEventListener('pointercancel',endLook);
    renderer.domElement.addEventListener('lostpointercapture',endLook);

    function holdButton(el, onDown, onUp=()=>{}) {
      if (!el) return;
      let activePointer=null;
      const release=e=>{
        if(activePointer===null || (e.pointerId!==undefined&&e.pointerId!==activePointer))return;
        activePointer=null;el.classList.remove('pressed');onUp();
      };
      el.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {
        e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();activePointer=e.pointerId;el.classList.add('pressed');
        try{el.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_){} onDown();
      });
      el.addEventListener('pointerup', release);
      el.addEventListener('pointercancel', release);
      el.addEventListener('lostpointercapture', release);
    }
    function tapButton(el,onTap){
      if(!el)return;
      el.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();el.classList.add('pressed');onTap();});
      const clear=()=>el.classList.remove('pressed');el.addEventListener('pointerup',clear);el.addEventListener('pointercancel',clear);el.addEventListener('pointerleave',clear);
    }
    holdButton($('fireBtn'), ()=>{primeSfxContext();firing=true;fireWeapon(performance.now()/1000);}, ()=>firing=false);
    tapButton($('jumpBtn'), jump);
    tapButton($('reloadBtn'), startReload);
    tapButton($('swapBtn'), cycleOwnedSlot);
    tapButton($('scopeBtn'), toggleAiming);
    holdButton($('useBtn'), ()=>{cancelRespawnProtection();setInputKey('KeyE',true);}, ()=>setInputKey('KeyE',false));
    holdButton($('crouchBtn'), ()=>{cancelRespawnProtection();setInputKey('ControlLeft',true);}, ()=>setInputKey('ControlLeft',false));
    tapButton($('buyBtn'), event=>toggleBuyMenu(event));
    tapButton($('pauseBtn'), event=>setPause(true,event));

    // ---------- Audio ----------
    // Low-latency architecture:
    // 1) create one interactive AudioContext;
    // 2) decode local embedded sounds while the menu is visible;
    // 3) decode optional CC0 firearm recordings into AudioBuffers;
    // 4) start a fresh AudioBufferSourceNode at the exact shot frame.
    // HTMLAudioElement pools were removed because network/media-element decoding
    // can make individual shots audibly late even when preload="auto" is set.
    let audioCtx = null, sfxBus = null, shotBus = null, shotLimiter = null, noiseBuffer = null, awpBoomBuffer = null;
    let audioKeepAliveSource = null, audioKeepAliveGain = null;

    const embeddedSfxBuffers = new Map();

    const actionSfxBuffers = new Map();

    let embeddedSfxPromise = null;

    let actionSfxPromise = null;
    let interfaceSfxInstalled = false;

    const activeGameplaySfxSources = new Set();
    const activeShotSources = new Set();
